# TIMER_TABLE_01 (STAGGER_DESIGN_06 section 9.6, audit A-6)

Author: Frontrunner (Sonnet 5.5), compiled from six parallel auditors' read-only passes. Reviewer: Haiku (a different model). Status: static, validated mechanically; semantic review by a different model still required. Base: git revision `6f1f7dfd6`; every line number is a line at that revision and must be re-checked at the implementation base (V-1). No code, build, game or test was run. Rows whose path starts with `engine/` (slice F, engine-owned bot modules) were read from the local engine checkout (ENGINE_VERSION 526bf98c42e741ebc2f6ccc0f8bcebedc6aaa177 in mod.config), not from a git object of this repository, so their lines are engine lines. Mechanical check (chk_snippets_01.py): of the repository rows, every snippet was located within 3 lines of its cited line at 6f1f7dfd6 except eight rows whose snippet abbreviates the code (B023, D032, D056, D079-D081, E078, E085), checked by hand and found at the cited lines. The check proves location, not classification.

The table, not a grep count, decides each site (design 9.6). Rule of doubt: a site that does not provably run unconditionally at its deadline is class C. This document gates every converted timer (design step 5): no timer code is converted before it and SAVE_TABLE_01 pass review.
